    /**
     * The shared fast-check parameters: 200 runs, or `FC_NUM_RUNS` when it is a positive
     * integer. No property file sets `numRuns` itself (testing.md).

    export function fcParams(): Parameters<unknown[]> {
      const requested = Number(process.env.FC_NUM_RUNS);
      const numRuns = Number.isInteger(requested) && requested > 0 ? requested : DEFAULT_NUM_RUNS;
      return { numRuns };
    }

    export function fcMakeParams(): Parameters<unknown[]> {
      const requested = Number(process.env.FC_MAKE_RUNS);
      const numRuns = Number.isInteger(requested) && requested > 0 ? requested : DEFAULT_MAKE_RUNS;
      return { numRuns };
    }

    import { Hono } from 'hono';

    /**
     * `GET /api/samples` (R3): every sample with its text, plan and issues. The body is built once;
     * the route reads no file, calls no model and consumes no limit.
     */
    export function createSamplesRoute(library: SampleLibrary): Hono {
      const body: SamplesResponse = {
        samples: library.samples.map((sample) => ({
          id: sample.id,
          title: sample.title,
          blurb: sample.blurb,
          dishes: sample.dishes,
          kitchen: sample.kitchen,
          servingTime: sample.servingTime,
          text: sample.text,
          plan: sample.plan,
          issues: sample.issues,
        })),
      };
      return new Hono().get('/', (c) => c.json(body));
    }

cat > test/web/state/clock.test.ts <<'EOF'
import { afterAll, beforeAll, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import {
  clockAt,
  defaultServing,
  earliestServing,
  formatClock,
  formatDay,
  formatDuration,
  formatWeekday,
  minuteAt,
  quarterCeil,
  quarterFloor,
  resolveServing,
  toServeTime,
} from '../../../src/web/state/clock.js';

const MIN = 60_000;
const at = (iso: string): number => Date.parse(iso);
const original = process.env['TZ'];

function setZone(zone: string): void {
  process.env['TZ'] = zone;
}

afterAll(() => {
  if (original === undefined) delete process.env['TZ'];
  else process.env['TZ'] = original;
});

describe('clock under Europe/London', () => {
  beforeAll(() => {
    setZone('Europe/London');
    // 2026-07-01 is BST (UTC+1): the offset in minutes is -60 only when the zone took effect.
    expect(new Date(2026, 6, 1).getTimezoneOffset()).toBe(-60);
    expect(new Date(2026, 10, 11).getTimezoneOffset()).toBe(0);
  });

  const WED_18 = at('2026-11-11T18:00:00Z');

  describe('resolveServing (R5.3)', () => {
    it('R5.3 a time later today stays today', () => {
      expect(resolveServing('19:30', WED_18)).toBe(at('2026-11-11T19:30:00Z'));
    });
    it('R5.3 a time exactly 4 h before now stays today', () => {
      expect(resolveServing('14:00', WED_18)).toBe(at('2026-11-11T14:00:00Z'));
    });
    it('R5.3 a time 4 h 1 min before now is tomorrow', () => {
      expect(resolveServing('13:59', WED_18)).toBe(at('2026-11-12T13:59:00Z'));
    });
    it('R5.3 00:30 is tomorrow', () => {
      expect(resolveServing('00:30', WED_18)).toBe(at('2026-11-12T00:30:00Z'));
    });
    it('R5.3 02:30 on the night the clocks go back is the GMT 02:30', () => {
      const now = at('2026-10-24T19:00:00Z'); // Sat 20:00 BST
      expect(resolveServing('02:30', now)).toBe(at('2026-10-25T02:30:00Z'));
    });
  });

  it('R5.5 minutes are real minutes across the DST change: 110 min before 02:30 GMT is 01:40', () => {
    const serving = at('2026-10-25T02:30:00Z');
    expect(formatClock(clockAt(serving, -110), 'en-GB')).toBe('01:40');
  });

  describe('defaultServing (R1.4)', () => {
    it('R1.4 the suggested time today when it is at least span + 15 min away', () => {
      const result = defaultServing(at('2026-11-11T10:00:00Z'), 110, '13:30');
      expect(result).toEqual({ time: '13:30', atMs: at('2026-11-11T13:30:00Z') });
    });
    it('R1.4 else the first :00 or :30 at or after now + span + 15 min', () => {
      const result = defaultServing(at('2026-11-11T12:00:00Z'), 110, '13:30');
      expect(result).toEqual({ time: '14:30', atMs: at('2026-11-11T14:30:00Z') });
    });
    it('R1.4 without a suggestion, past midnight', () => {
      const result = defaultServing(at('2026-11-11T22:50:00Z'), 110, null);
      expect(result).toEqual({ time: '01:00', atMs: at('2026-11-12T01:00:00Z') });
    });
    it('R1.4 now is rounded up to the next minute first', () => {
      const result = defaultServing(at('2026-11-11T18:00:20Z'), 110, null);
      expect(result).toEqual({ time: '20:30', atMs: at('2026-11-11T20:30:00Z') });
    });
  });

  it('R5.5 earliestServing rounds now + span up to 5 minutes', () => {
    expect(earliestServing(at('2026-11-11T17:41:10Z'), 110)).toBe(at('2026-11-11T19:35:00Z'));
  });

  it('toServeTime writes the local HH:MM', () => {
    expect(toServeTime(at('2026-07-01T18:05:00Z'))).toBe('19:05'); // BST
  });

  describe('formatting', () => {
    it('R7.4 formatDuration', () => {
      expect([1, 60, 75, 110, 1440, 45].map(formatDuration)).toEqual([
        '1 min',
        '1 h',
        '1 h 15 min',
        '1 h 50 min',
        '24 h',
        '45 min',
      ]);
    });
    it('formatClock en-GB is 24 h', () => {
      expect(formatClock(at('2026-11-11T19:30:00Z'), 'en-GB')).toBe('19:30');
    });
    it('formatClock en-US is 12 h with a space before PM', () => {
      const text = formatClock(at('2026-11-11T19:30:00Z'), 'en-US').replace(/[\u202f\u00a0]/g, ' ');
      expect(text).toBe('7:30 PM');
    });
    it('formatWeekday is the short weekday', () => {
      expect(formatWeekday(at('2026-11-11T19:30:00Z'), 'en-GB')).toBe('Wed');
    });
    it('R5.4 formatDay: today null, tomorrow, yesterday, and a date two days away', () => {
      const now = at('2026-11-11T18:00:00Z');
      expect(formatDay(at('2026-11-11T19:30:00Z'), now, 'en-GB')).toBeNull();
      expect(formatDay(at('2026-11-12T00:30:00Z'), now, 'en-GB')).toBe('tomorrow');
      expect(formatDay(at('2026-11-10T23:00:00Z'), now, 'en-GB')).toBe('yesterday');
      expect(formatDay(at('2026-11-13T09:00:00Z'), now, 'en-GB')).toBe('Fri 13 Nov');
    });
  });

  describe('minutes and quarter hours', () => {
    const chosen = at('2026-11-11T19:30:00Z');
    it('R6.3 minuteAt floors negative differences', () => {
      expect(minuteAt(chosen, chosen - 90 * MIN)).toBe(-90);
      expect(minuteAt(chosen, chosen - 90 * MIN - 1)).toBe(-91);
      expect(minuteAt(chosen, chosen + 59_999)).toBe(0);
      expect(minuteAt(chosen, chosen + 60_000)).toBe(1);
    });
    it('R6.3 quarterFloor and quarterCeil on a quarter hour return the minute', () => {
      expect(quarterFloor(chosen, -90)).toBe(-90);
      expect(quarterCeil(chosen, -90)).toBe(-90);
      expect(quarterFloor(chosen, 0)).toBe(0);
    });
    it('R6.3 off a quarter hour: 17:40 floors to 17:30 and 19:32 ceils to 19:45', () => {
      expect(quarterFloor(chosen, -110)).toBe(-120);
      expect(quarterCeil(chosen, 2)).toBe(15);
      expect(quarterCeil(chosen, -118)).toBe(-105);
    });
  });
});

describe('clock under Asia/Kathmandu (+5:45)', () => {
  beforeAll(() => {
    setZone('Asia/Kathmandu');
    expect(new Date(2026, 10, 11).getTimezoneOffset()).toBe(-345);
  });

  it('R6.3 quarter hours are local: 19:30 Kathmandu is 13:45Z, and 3 min before is not a quarter', () => {
    const chosen = at('2026-11-11T13:45:00Z'); // 19:30 local
    expect(formatClock(chosen, 'en-GB')).toBe('19:30');
    expect(quarterFloor(chosen, -3)).toBe(-15);
    expect(quarterCeil(chosen, -17)).toBe(-15);
    expect(quarterFloor(chosen, -15)).toBe(-15);
  });
});

[tool:execute] $ cd ~/hackathons-2026/kiroween/makedinner && cat > src/web/state/clock.ts <<'EOF'
import type { ServeTime } from '../../core/index.js';

/**
 * The time mapping of plan-ui (design, "Time"). Minutes are real minutes: `clockAt` adds
 * milliseconds, so a plan across a DST change stays physically right. Local-time work uses
 * the runtime's time zone through `Date` local methods; formatting uses `Intl.DateTimeFormat`
 * with an undefined locale meaning the user's.
 */
export const MINUTE_MS = 60_000;
const HOUR_MS = 60 * MINUTE_MS;

export interface Clock {
  nowMs(): number;
}

/** The chosen serving time: minute 0 of the axis for the whole meal. */
export interface Serving {
  time: ServeTime;
  atMs: number;
}

/** Reads the system clock. Used only by effects and `useNow`. */
export const systemClock: Clock = { nowMs: () => Date.now() };

export function clockAt(chosenMs: number, minute: number): number {
  return chosenMs + minute * MINUTE_MS;
}

export function minuteAt(chosenMs: number, nowMs: number): number {
  return Math.floor((nowMs - chosenMs) / MINUTE_MS);
}

function ceilMinute(ms: number): number {
  return Math.ceil(ms / MINUTE_MS) * MINUTE_MS;
}

/** The first instant at that local clock time at or after 4 hours before `nowMs` (R5.3). */
export function resolveServing(time: ServeTime, nowMs: number): number {
  const hours = Number(time.slice(0, 2));
  const minutes = Number(time.slice(3, 5));
  const today = new Date(nowMs);
  const floor = nowMs - 4 * HOUR_MS;
  let latest = 0;
  for (let k = -1; k <= 2; k += 1) {
    const candidate = new Date(
      today.getFullYear(),
      today.getMonth(),
      today.getDate() + k,
      hours,
      minutes,
    ).getTime();
    latest = candidate;
    if (candidate >= floor) return candidate;
  }
  return latest;
}

export function toServeTime(ms: number): ServeTime {
  const date = new Date(ms);
  const pad = (n: number): string => String(n).padStart(2, '0');
  return `${pad(date.getHours())}:${pad(date.getMinutes())}`;
}

/** The first local :00 or :30 at or after `ms` (which is minute-aligned). */
function nextHalfHour(ms: number): number {
  const minutes = new Date(ms).getMinutes();
  return ms + ((30 - (minutes % 30)) % 30) * MINUTE_MS;
}

export function defaultServing(
  nowMs: number,
  spanMin: number,
  suggested: ServeTime | null,
): Serving {
  const need = ceilMinute(nowMs) + (spanMin + 15) * MINUTE_MS;
  if (suggested !== null) {
    const hours = Number(suggested.slice(0, 2));
    const minutes = Number(suggested.slice(3, 5));
    const today = new Date(nowMs);
    const candidate = new Date(
      today.getFullYear(),
      today.getMonth(),
      today.getDate(),
      hours,
      minutes,
    ).getTime();
    if (candidate >= need) return { time: suggested, atMs: candidate };
  }
  const atMs = nextHalfHour(need);
  return { time: toServeTime(atMs), atMs };
}

export function earliestServing(nowMs: number, spanMin: number): number {
  const base = ceilMinute(nowMs) + spanMin * MINUTE_MS;
  const minutes = new Date(base).getMinutes();
  return base + ((5 - (minutes % 5)) % 5) * MINUTE_MS;
}

/** Largest minute `<= minute` that falls on a local quarter hour. */
export function quarterFloor(chosenMs: number, minute: number): number {
  const local = new Date(clockAt(chosenMs, minute)).getMinutes();
  return minute - (local % 15);
}

/** Smallest minute `>= minute` that falls on a local quarter hour. */
export function quarterCeil(chosenMs: number, minute: number): number {
  const local = new Date(clockAt(chosenMs, minute)).getMinutes();
  return minute + ((15 - (local % 15)) % 15);
}

export function sameLocalDate(a: number, b: number): boolean {
  const x = new Date(a);
  const y = new Date(b);
  return (
    x.getFullYear() === y.getFullYear() &&
    x.getMonth() === y.getMonth() &&
    x.getDate() === y.getDate()
  );
}

export function formatClock(ms: number, locale?: string): string {
  return new Intl.DateTimeFormat(locale, { timeStyle: 'short' }).format(ms);
}

export function formatWeekday(ms: number, locale?: string): string {
  return new Intl.DateTimeFormat(locale, { weekday: 'short' }).format(ms);
}

/** `null` for today, "tomorrow", "yesterday", else a short date like "Fri 13 Nov". */
export function formatDay(ms: number, nowMs: number, locale?: string): string | null {
  if (sameLocalDate(ms, nowMs)) return null;
  const now = new Date(nowMs);
  const tomorrow = new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth(), now.getDate() + 1).getTime();
  const yesterday = new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth(), now.getDate() - 1).getTime();
  if (sameLocalDate(ms, tomorrow)) return 'tomorrow';
  if (sameLocalDate(ms, yesterday)) return 'yesterday';
  return new Intl.DateTimeFormat(locale, {
    weekday: 'short',
    day: 'numeric',
    month: 'short',
  })
    .format(ms)
    .replace(',', '');
}

/** "1 min", "45 min", "1 h", "1 h 50 min". */
export function formatDuration(minutes: number): string {
  const whole = Math.max(0, Math.round(minutes));
  const hours = Math.floor(whole / 60);
  const rest = whole % 60;
  if (hours === 0) return `${rest} min`;
  return rest === 0 ? `${hours} h` : `${hours} h ${rest} min`;
}
EOF

    import { fcParams } from '../support/fc-params.js';
    import {
      decodeEchoLine,
      oracleDinnerfile,
      oracleEchoText,
      oracleMakeLines,
    } from '../support/oracles.js';
        .array(fc.constantFrom(...HOSTILE_TOKENS), { minLength: 1, maxLength: 12 })
        .map((tokens) => [...tokens.join('')].slice(0, max).join(''))
        .filter((text) => /\S/.test(text));
    }
    
    /** A serving time `HH:MM`, hour 0..23 and minute 0..59, zero-padded. */
    export function arbServeTime(): fc.Arbitrary<string> {
      return fc
        .tuple(fc.integer({ min: 0, max: 23 }), fc.integer({ min: 0, max: 59 }))
        .map(([h, m]) => `${String(h).padStart(2, '0')}:${String(m).padStart(2, '0')}`);
    }
    
    /** Options with a valid kitchen and serving time; each key present or absent, never `undefined`. */
    export function arbDinnerfileOptions(): fc.Arbitrary<DinnerfileOptions> {
      return fc.record({ kitchen: arbKitchen(), serve: arbServeTime() }, { requiredKeys: [] });
    }

import fc from 'fast-check';
import { afterAll, beforeAll, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import type { ServeTime } from '../../../src/core/index.js';
import {
  clockAt,
  minuteAt,
  resolveServing,
  toServeTime,
} from '../../../src/web/state/clock.js';
import { arbServeTime } from '../../support/arbitraries.js';
import { fcParams } from '../../support/fc-params.js';

const HOUR = 3_600_000;
const original = process.env['TZ'];

describe('clock properties under Europe/London', () => {
  beforeAll(() => {
    process.env['TZ'] = 'Europe/London';
    expect(new Date(2026, 6, 1).getTimezoneOffset()).toBe(-60);
  });
  afterAll(() => {
    if (original === undefined) delete process.env['TZ'];
    else process.env['TZ'] = original;
  });

  const arbNowMs = fc.integer({
    min: Date.UTC(2026, 0, 1),
    max: Date.UTC(2027, 11, 31),
  });

  it('P-UI5 minuteAt inverts clockAt for every integer minute', () => {
    fc.assert(
      fc.property(
        arbNowMs,
        fc.integer({ min: -2000, max: 2000 }),
        (chosenMs, minute) => minuteAt(chosenMs, clockAt(chosenMs, minute)) === minute,
      ),
      fcParams(),
    );
  });

  it('P-UI5 resolveServing lies in [now - 4 h, now + 21 h] and keeps the clock time outside DST gaps', () => {
    fc.assert(
      fc.property(arbNowMs, arbServeTime(), (nowMs, time) => {
        const result = resolveServing(time as ServeTime, nowMs);
        expect(result).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(nowMs - 4 * HOUR);
        expect(result).toBeLessThanOrEqual(nowMs + 21 * HOUR);
        const nearChange =
          new Date(result - 3 * HOUR).getTimezoneOffset() !==
          new Date(result + 3 * HOUR).getTimezoneOffset();
        if (!nearChange) expect(toServeTime(result)).toBe(time);
      }),
      fcParams(),
    );
  });
});
EOF

import { z } from 'zod';
import {
  DishIdSchema,
  IssueSchema,
  KitchenSchema,
  PlanSchema,
  ServeTimeSchema,
} from '../../core/index.js';
import type { Result } from '../../core/index.js';

/*
 * Response schemas of the two endpoints the browser uses (plan-ui design, "API").
 * `z.object`, not `z.strictObject`: a field the server adds later is dropped, not fatal.
 */
export const SampleItemSchema = z.object({
  id: DishIdSchema,
  title: z.string().min(1).max(80),
  blurb: z.string().min(1).max(160),
  dishes: z.array(z.string().min(1).max(80)).min(1).max(12),
  kitchen: KitchenSchema,
  servingTime: ServeTimeSchema,
  text: z.string(),
  plan: PlanSchema,
  issues: z.array(IssueSchema),
});
export const SamplesResponseSchema = z.object({
  samples: z.array(SampleItemSchema).min(1).max(12),
});

export type SampleItem = z.infer<typeof SampleItemSchema>;

/** Why a parse request gave no plan (T6 fills in `postParse`). */
export type ParseFailure =
  | {
      kind:
        | 'bad_request'
        | 'input_too_long'
        | 'model_unavailable'
        | 'model_refused'
        | 'model_output_invalid'
        | 'internal'
        | 'network';
    }
  | { kind: 'rate_limited' | 'daily_cap_reached'; retryAtMs: number | null }
  | { kind: 'unexpected'; status: number };

export type FetchLike = (url: string, init?: RequestInit) => Promise<Response>;

export function fetchSamples(
  _fetchFn: FetchLike,
): Promise<Result<SampleItem[], 'network' | 'unexpected'>> {
  return Promise.resolve({ ok: false, error: 'unexpected' });
}

[tool:execute] $ cd ~/hackathons-2026/kiroween/makedinner && cat > src/web/state/store.ts <<'EOF'
import { schedule, validatePlan } from '../../core/index.js';
import type {
  Kitchen,
  Plan,
  PlanCheck,
  Result,
  Schedule,
  ScheduleError,
  ServeTime,
} from '../../core/index.js';
import type { ImportProblemKind, ParseFailure, SampleItem } from './api.js';
import type { CookAction, CookState } from './cook-slot.js';
import { reduceCook } from './cook-slot.js';
import type { Serving } from './clock.js';

export type { Serving } from './clock.js';

export type Route = 'menu' | 'steps' | 'plan' | 'cook';
export type ThemeChoice = 'system' | 'light' | 'dark';
export type PlanSource =
  | { kind: 'sample'; sampleId: string }
  | { kind: 'recipes' }
  | { kind: 'dinnerfile' }
  | { kind: 'share' };

export interface Dinner {
  plan: Plan;
  source: PlanSource;
  kitchen: Kitchen;
  serving: Serving | null;
}

export interface DinnerLoad {
  plan: Plan;
  source: PlanSource;
  kitchen: Kitchen;
  /** A fixed time (share, Dinnerfile), resolved by `resolveServing`. */
  serve: ServeTime | null;
  /** A sample's `servingTime`, used by `defaultServing`. */
  suggested: ServeTime | null;
  route: 'steps' | 'plan';
  notice: string | null;
}

export type SamplesState =
  | { status: 'idle' | 'loading' | 'failed' }
  | { status: 'loaded'; samples: SampleItem[] };
export type ParseState =
  | { status: 'idle' }
  | { status: 'busy'; requestId: number; startedAtMs: number }
  | { status: 'failed'; failure: ParseFailure };
export type ImportProblem = ImportProblemKind;
export type ImportState =
  | { status: 'idle' }
  | { status: 'failed'; heading: string; problems: ImportProblem[] };

export interface AppState {
  route: Route;
  theme: ThemeChoice;
  /** The plan always passes `PlanSchema` (R4.4). */
  dinner: Dinner | null;
  recipeText: string;
  dinnerfileText: string;
  samples: SamplesState;
  parse: ParseState;
  import: ImportState;
  pendingLoad: DinnerLoad | null;
  notice: string | null;
  /** Owned by cooking-mode; always null in plan-ui. */
  cook: CookState | null;
}

export const DEFAULT_KITCHEN: Kitchen = { burners: 4, ovens: 1, cooks: 1 };

export function initialState(): AppState {
  return {
    route: 'menu',
    theme: 'system',
    dinner: null,
    recipeText: '',
    dinnerfileText: '',
    samples: { status: 'idle' },
    parse: { status: 'idle' },
    import: { status: 'idle' },
    pendingLoad: null,
    notice: null,
    cook: null,
  };
}

export type PlanUiAction =
  | { type: 'navigate'; route: Route; cookRegistered: boolean }
  | { type: 'theme/set'; theme: ThemeChoice }
  | { type: 'notice/set'; text: string }
  | { type: 'notice/clear' }
  | { type: 'start-over' };

export type Action = PlanUiAction | CookAction;

export function reduce(state: AppState, _action: Action): AppState {
  return state;
}

export interface Store {
  getState(): AppState;
  dispatch(action: Action): void;
  subscribe(listener: () => void): () => void;
}

export function createStore(initial: AppState): Store {
  let state = initial;
  return {
    getState: () => state,
    dispatch: () => undefined,
    subscribe: () => () => undefined,
  };
}

export function selectCheck(_state: AppState): PlanCheck | null {
  void validatePlan;
  return null;
}

export function selectSchedule(_state: AppState): Result<Schedule, ScheduleError> | null {
  void schedule;
  return null;
}

export type Reachability = Record<Route, { ok: true } | { ok: false; reason: string }>;

export function reachable(_state: AppState, _cookRegistered: boolean): Reachability {
  const no = { ok: false, reason: '' } as const;
  return { menu: no, steps: no, plan: no, cook: no };
}

void reduceCook;

[tool:execute] $ cd ~/hackathons-2026/kiroween/makedinner && cat > test/web/state/store.test.ts <<'EOF'
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import type { Plan } from '../../../src/core/index.js';
import {
  createStore,
  initialState,
  reachable,
  reduce,
  selectCheck,
  selectSchedule,
} from '../../../src/web/state/store.js';
import type { Action, AppState, Dinner } from '../../../src/web/state/store.js';
import { HOME_KITCHEN, SUNDAY_ROAST } from '../../support/fixtures.js';

/** SUNDAY_ROAST with `gravy.roux` waiting for a step that does not exist: an unknown_dependency error. */
const BROKEN_ROAST: Plan = {
  ...SUNDAY_ROAST,
  steps: SUNDAY_ROAST.steps.map((step) =>
    step.id === 'gravy.roux' ? { ...step, after: ['chicken.rost'] } : step,
  ),
};

function dinnerOf(plan: Plan): Dinner {
  return { plan, source: { kind: 'sample', sampleId: 'sunday-roast' }, kitchen: HOME_KITCHEN, serving: null };
}

function stateWith(plan: Plan | null, patch: Partial<AppState> = {}): AppState {
  return { ...initialState(), dinner: plan === null ? null : dinnerOf(plan), ...patch };
}

describe('initialState', () => {
  it('R9.2 starts on the Menu with nothing loaded', () => {
    expect(initialState()).toEqual({
      route: 'menu',
      theme: 'system',
      dinner: null,
      recipeText: '',
      dinnerfileText: '',
      samples: { status: 'idle' },
      parse: { status: 'idle' },
      import: { status: 'idle' },
      pendingLoad: null,
      notice: null,
      cook: null,
    });
  });
});

describe('reachable (R9.2 table)', () => {
  it('menu is always reachable; the others need a dinner', () => {
    expect(reachable(initialState(), false)).toEqual({
      menu: { ok: true },
      steps: { ok: false, reason: 'Pick a dinner first' },
      plan: { ok: false, reason: 'Pick a dinner first' },
      cook: { ok: false, reason: 'Cooking mode is not available yet' },
    });
  });
  it('a plan with an error reaches Steps but not Plan', () => {
    expect(reachable(stateWith(BROKEN_ROAST), false)).toEqual({
      menu: { ok: true },
      steps: { ok: true },
      plan: { ok: false, reason: 'Fix the errors in Steps first' },
      cook: { ok: false, reason: 'Cooking mode is not available yet' },
    });
  });
  it('a valid plan reaches Plan; Cook only when registered', () => {
    expect(reachable(stateWith(SUNDAY_ROAST), false).cook).toEqual({
      ok: false,
      reason: 'Cooking mode is not available yet',
    });
    expect(reachable(stateWith(SUNDAY_ROAST), true)).toEqual({
      menu: { ok: true },
      steps: { ok: true },
      plan: { ok: true },
      cook: { ok: true },
    });
  });
  it('Cook is unreachable without a schedulable plan even when registered', () => {
    expect(reachable(initialState(), true).cook).toEqual({
      ok: false,
      reason: 'Make the plan first',
    });
    expect(reachable(stateWith(BROKEN_ROAST), true).cook).toEqual({
      ok: false,
      reason: 'Make the plan first',
    });
  });
});

describe('selectors', () => {
  it('selectCheck is null without a dinner and reports the unknown dependency', () => {
    expect(selectCheck(initialState())).toBeNull();
    const check = selectCheck(stateWith(BROKEN_ROAST));
    expect(check?.issues.map((i) => i.code)).toEqual(['unknown_dependency']);
  });
  it('selectCheck returns the same object for the same plan', () => {
    const state = stateWith(SUNDAY_ROAST);
    expect(selectCheck(state)).toBe(selectCheck({ ...state, route: 'steps' }));
  });
  it('selectSchedule is null without a dinner; ok with a span of 110 for the roast', () => {
    expect(selectSchedule(initialState())).toBeNull();
    const result = selectSchedule(stateWith(SUNDAY_ROAST));
    expect(result?.ok).toBe(true);
    expect(result?.ok === true ? result.value.spanMin : null).toBe(110);
  });
});

describe('reduce: navigate (R9.3)', () => {
  const go = (state: AppState, route: AppState['route'], cookRegistered = false): AppState =>
    reduce(state, { type: 'navigate', route, cookRegistered });

  it('with no dinner every route but menu lands on menu', () => {
    for (const route of ['steps', 'plan', 'cook'] as const) {
      expect(go(initialState(), route).route).toBe('menu');
    }
  });
  it('with a valid dinner steps and plan are reached; cook falls back to plan when unregistered', () => {
    const state = stateWith(SUNDAY_ROAST);
    expect(go(state, 'steps').route).toBe('steps');
    expect(go(state, 'plan').route).toBe('plan');
    expect(go(state, 'cook', false).route).toBe('plan');
    expect(go(state, 'cook', true).route).toBe('cook');
  });
  it('with an error in the plan, plan and cook fall back to steps', () => {
    const state = stateWith(BROKEN_ROAST);
    expect(go(state, 'plan').route).toBe('steps');
    expect(go(state, 'cook', true).route).toBe('steps');
  });
  it('clears the notice', () => {
    const state = stateWith(SUNDAY_ROAST, { notice: 'Removed a step.' });
    expect(go(state, 'steps').notice).toBeNull();
  });
});

describe('reduce: small actions', () => {
  it('theme/set stores the choice', () => {
    expect(reduce(initialState(), { type: 'theme/set', theme: 'dark' }).theme).toBe('dark');
  });
  it('notice/set and notice/clear', () => {
    const set = reduce(initialState(), { type: 'notice/set', text: 'Opened a shared dinner.' });
    expect(set.notice).toBe('Opened a shared dinner.');
    expect(reduce(set, { type: 'notice/clear' }).notice).toBeNull();
  });
  it('R9.7 start-over keeps theme and samples and clears the rest', () => {
    const samples = { status: 'failed' } as const;
    const state = stateWith(SUNDAY_ROAST, {
      route: 'plan',
      theme: 'dark',
      samples,
      recipeText: 'Some recipes',
      dinnerfileText: 'dinner:',
      notice: 'x',
    });
    const next = reduce(state, { type: 'start-over' });
    expect(next).toEqual({ ...initialState(), theme: 'dark', samples });
  });
  it('a no-op returns the same object', () => {
    const state = stateWith(SUNDAY_ROAST, { theme: 'dark', route: 'plan' });
    expect(reduce(state, { type: 'theme/set', theme: 'dark' })).toBe(state);
    expect(reduce(state, { type: 'notice/clear' })).toBe(state);
    expect(reduce(state, { type: 'navigate', route: 'plan', cookRegistered: false })).toBe(state);
  });
  it('R9.9 an action outside PlanUiAction reaches reduceCook and changes nothing', () => {
    const state = stateWith(SUNDAY_ROAST);
    const foreign = { type: 'cook/begin' } as unknown as Action;
    expect(reduce(state, foreign)).toBe(state);
  });
});

describe('createStore', () => {
  it('notifies once per change and never for a no-op', () => {
    const store = createStore(initialState());
    let calls = 0;
    const unsubscribe = store.subscribe(() => {
      calls += 1;
    });
    store.dispatch({ type: 'theme/set', theme: 'dark' });
    expect(calls).toBe(1);
    expect(store.getState().theme).toBe('dark');
    store.dispatch({ type: 'theme/set', theme: 'dark' });
    expect(calls).toBe(1);
    unsubscribe();
    store.dispatch({ type: 'theme/set', theme: 'light' });
    expect(calls).toBe(1);
    expect(store.getState().theme).toBe('light');
  });
});
EOF

import re
p='src/web/state/store.ts'
s=open(p).read()
i=s.index('export function reduce(')
head=s[:i]
head=head.replace("import type { Serving } from './clock.js';\n","import type { Serving } from './clock.js';\nimport { COPY } from '../copy.js';\n")
new = '''const ROUTE_ORDER: readonly Route[] = ['menu', 'steps', 'plan', 'cook'];

/** Plan-ui reducers; every other action goes to `reduceCook` (R9.9). */
export function reduce(state: AppState, action: Action): AppState {
  switch (action.type) {
    case 'navigate': {
      const reach = reachable(state, action.cookRegistered);
      let index = ROUTE_ORDER.indexOf(action.route);
      while (index > 0 && !reach[ROUTE_ORDER[index] ?? 'menu'].ok) index -= 1;
      const route = ROUTE_ORDER[index] ?? 'menu';
      if (route === state.route && state.notice === null) return state;
      return { ...state, route, notice: null };
    }
    case 'theme/set':
      return action.theme === state.theme ? state : { ...state, theme: action.theme };
    case 'notice/set':
      return action.text === state.notice ? state : { ...state, notice: action.text };
    case 'notice/clear':
      return state.notice === null ? state : { ...state, notice: null };
    case 'start-over':
      return { ...initialState(), theme: state.theme, samples: state.samples };
    default:
      return reduceCook(state, action);
  }
}

export interface Store {
  getState(): AppState;
  dispatch(action: Action): void;
  subscribe(listener: () => void): () => void;
}

/** Calls `reduce`; notifies subscribers only when the state object changed. */
export function createStore(initial: AppState): Store {
  let state = initial;
  const listeners = new Set<() => void>();
  return {
    getState: () => state,
    dispatch(action) {
      const next = reduce(state, action);
      if (next === state) return;
      state = next;
      for (const listener of [...listeners]) listener();
    },
    subscribe(listener) {
      listeners.add(listener);
      return () => {
        listeners.delete(listener);
      };
    },
  };
}

// One-entry memos keyed by object identity: dinner.plan and dinner.kitchen are replaced,
// never mutated.
let checkMemo: { plan: Plan; value: PlanCheck } | null = null;
let scheduleMemo: {
  plan: Plan;
  kitchen: Kitchen;
  value: Result<Schedule, ScheduleError>;
} | null = null;

/** `validatePlan` of the current plan, or null with no dinner. */
export function selectCheck(state: AppState): PlanCheck | null {
  if (state.dinner === null) return null;
  const { plan } = state.dinner;
  if (checkMemo?.plan !== plan) checkMemo = { plan, value: validatePlan(plan) };
  return checkMemo.value;
}

/** `schedule(plan, kitchen)` of the current dinner, or null with no dinner. */
export function selectSchedule(state: AppState): Result<Schedule, ScheduleError> | null {
  if (state.dinner === null) return null;
  const { plan, kitchen } = state.dinner;
  if (scheduleMemo?.plan !== plan || scheduleMemo.kitchen !== kitchen) {
    scheduleMemo = { plan, kitchen, value: schedule(plan, kitchen) };
  }
  return scheduleMemo.value;
}

export type Reachability = Record<Route, { ok: true } | { ok: false; reason: string }>;

export function reachable(state: AppState, cookRegistered: boolean): Reachability {
  const ok = { ok: true } as const;
  const no = (reason: string): { ok: false; reason: string } => ({ ok: false, reason });
  const check = selectCheck(state);
  const planReady = check !== null && !hasErrors(check.issues);
  const scheduled = selectSchedule(state);
  let cook: Reachability['cook'] = ok;
  if (!cookRegistered) cook = no(COPY.reasonCookMissing);
  else if (scheduled === null || !scheduled.ok) cook = no(COPY.reasonMakePlan);
  return {
    menu: ok,
    steps: state.dinner === null ? no(COPY.reasonPickDinner) : ok,
    plan:
      state.dinner === null
        ? no(COPY.reasonPickDinner)
        : planReady
          ? ok
          : no(COPY.reasonFixErrors),
    cook,
  };
}
'''
head=head.replace("import { schedule, validatePlan } from","import { hasErrors, schedule, validatePlan } from")
open(p,'w').write(head+new)
EOF

cat > test/web/state/route.test.ts <<'EOF'
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { hashOf, parseHash } from '../../../src/web/state/route.js';

describe('parseHash (R9.3)', () => {
  it('an empty fragment is none', () => {
    expect(parseHash('')).toEqual({ kind: 'none' });
    expect(parseHash('#')).toEqual({ kind: 'none' });
    expect(parseHash('#/')).toEqual({ kind: 'none' });
  });
  it('the four routes', () => {
    for (const route of ['menu', 'steps', 'plan', 'cook'] as const) {
      expect(parseHash(`#/${route}`)).toEqual({ kind: 'route', route });
    }
  });
  it('anything else is unknown', () => {
    expect(parseHash('#/nope')).toEqual({ kind: 'unknown' });
    expect(parseHash('#/plan/')).toEqual({ kind: 'unknown' });
    expect(parseHash('plan')).toEqual({ kind: 'unknown' });
  });
  it('R8.5 a share fragment carries its version and payload', () => {
    expect(parseHash('#/share/1.abc_-XYZ09')).toEqual({
      kind: 'share',
      version: 1,
      payload: 'abc_-XYZ09',
    });
    expect(parseHash('#/share/12.a')).toEqual({ kind: 'share', version: 12, payload: 'a' });
  });
  it('R8.5 a share payload of 65,536 characters is accepted and 65,537 is unknown', () => {
    expect(parseHash(`#/share/1.${'a'.repeat(65_536)}`)).toEqual({
      kind: 'share',
      version: 1,
      payload: 'a'.repeat(65_536),
    });
    expect(parseHash(`#/share/1.${'a'.repeat(65_537)}`)).toEqual({ kind: 'unknown' });
  });
  it('R8.6 a share fragment with a bad character, no payload or a four-digit version is unknown', () => {
    expect(parseHash('#/share/1.ab*c')).toEqual({ kind: 'unknown' });
    expect(parseHash('#/share/1.')).toEqual({ kind: 'unknown' });
    expect(parseHash('#/share/1234.abc')).toEqual({ kind: 'unknown' });
    expect(parseHash('#/share/abc')).toEqual({ kind: 'unknown' });
  });
});

describe('hashOf', () => {
  it('is #/ plus the route', () => {
    expect(hashOf('plan')).toBe('#/plan');
    expect(hashOf('menu')).toBe('#/menu');
  });
});
EOF
cat > test/web/state/persist.test.ts <<'EOF'
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { loadState, saveState, STATE_KEY, toPersisted } from '../../../src/web/state/persist.js';
import { initialState } from '../../../src/web/state/store.js';
import type { AppState } from '../../../src/web/state/store.js';
import { HOME_KITCHEN, SUNDAY_ROAST } from '../../support/fixtures.js';

const UNREADABLE = 'Your saved dinner could not be read, so the app started fresh.';

function storageOf(value: string | null | 'throw'): Pick<Storage, 'getItem'> {
  return {
    getItem: (key) => {
      expect(key).toBe(STATE_KEY);
      if (value === 'throw') throw new Error('blocked');
      return value;
    },
  };
}

const SAVED: AppState = {
  ...initialState(),
  route: 'plan',
  theme: 'dark',
  recipeText: 'Roast a chicken.',
  dinnerfileText: '# plan "x"',
  dinner: {
    plan: SUNDAY_ROAST,
    source: { kind: 'sample', sampleId: 'sunday-roast' },
    kitchen: HOME_KITCHEN,
    serving: { time: '19:30', atMs: 1_762_889_400_000 },
  },
};

describe('loadState (R9.5, R9.6)', () => {
  it('R9.5 a missing key starts fresh without a notice', () => {
    expect(loadState(storageOf(null), false)).toEqual({ state: initialState(), notice: null });
  });
  it('R9.6 getItem throwing starts fresh with the notice', () => {
    expect(loadState(storageOf('throw'), false)).toEqual({
      state: initialState(),
      notice: UNREADABLE,
    });
  });
  it('R9.6 bad JSON starts fresh with the notice', () => {
    expect(loadState(storageOf('{not json'), false)).toEqual({
      state: initialState(),
      notice: UNREADABLE,
    });
  });
  it('R9.6 a schema failure starts fresh with the notice', () => {
    const stored = JSON.stringify({ ...toPersisted(SAVED), version: 2 });
    expect(loadState(storageOf(stored), false)).toEqual({
      state: initialState(),
      notice: UNREADABLE,
    });
  });
  it('R9.6 a plan failing validatePlan starts fresh with the notice', () => {
    const persisted = toPersisted(SAVED);
    const stored = JSON.stringify({
      ...persisted,
      dinner: { ...persisted.dinner, plan: { title: 'x', dishes: [], steps: [] } },
    });
    expect(loadState(storageOf(stored), false)).toEqual({
      state: initialState(),
      notice: UNREADABLE,
    });
  });
  it('R9.5 toPersisted then loadState gives the persisted fields back', () => {
    const stored = JSON.stringify(toPersisted(SAVED));
    const loaded = loadState(storageOf(stored), false);
    expect(loaded.notice).toBeNull();
    expect(loaded.state).toEqual(SAVED);
  });
  it('R9.5 a plan with an error is kept and returns to Steps', () => {
    const broken: AppState = {
      ...SAVED,
      dinner: {
        ...SAVED.dinner!,
        plan: {
          ...SUNDAY_ROAST,
          steps: SUNDAY_ROAST.steps.map((s) =>
            s.id === 'gravy.roux' ? { ...s, after: ['chicken.rost'] } : s,
          ),
        },
      },
    };
    const loaded = loadState(storageOf(JSON.stringify(toPersisted(broken))), false);
    expect(loaded.notice).toBeNull();
    expect(loaded.state.route).toBe('steps');
    expect(loaded.state.dinner?.plan.steps.find((s) => s.id === 'gravy.roux')?.after).toEqual([
      'chicken.rost',
    ]);
  });
  it('R9.3 a stored cook route without a cook screen comes back as plan', () => {
    const stored = JSON.stringify({ ...toPersisted(SAVED), route: 'cook' });
    expect(loadState(storageOf(stored), false).state.route).toBe('plan');
  });
});

describe('saveState (R9.6)', () => {
  it('writes the persisted state under the key and returns true', () => {
    const written: Array<[string, string]> = [];
    const ok = saveState({ setItem: (k, v) => void written.push([k, v]) }, SAVED);
    expect(ok).toBe(true);
    expect(written).toEqual([[STATE_KEY, JSON.stringify(toPersisted(SAVED))]]);
  });
  it('returns false when setItem throws', () => {
    const ok = saveState(
      {
        setItem: () => {
          throw new Error('quota');
        },
      },
      SAVED,
    );
    expect(ok).toBe(false);
  });
});
EOF

# route.ts
open('src/web/state/route.ts','w').write('''import type { Route } from './store.js';

export type HashTarget =
  | { kind: 'none' }
  | { kind: 'route'; route: Route }
  | { kind: 'share'; version: number; payload: string }
  | { kind: 'unknown' };

const ROUTES: readonly Route[] = ['menu', 'steps', 'plan', 'cook'];
const SHARE_PATTERN = /^#\\/share\\/([0-9]{1,3})\\.([A-Za-z0-9_-]{1,65536})$/;

/** Reads `location.hash`: '', '#', '#/' are none; '#/plan' a route; '#/share/1.<b64url>' a share. */
export function parseHash(hash: string): HashTarget {
  if (hash === '' || hash === '#' || hash === '#/') return { kind: 'none' };
  const route = ROUTES.find((r) => hash === `#/${r}`);
  if (route !== undefined) return { kind: 'route', route };
  const share = SHARE_PATTERN.exec(hash);
  if (share !== null) {
    return { kind: 'share', version: Number(share[1]), payload: share[2] ?? '' };
  }
  return { kind: 'unknown' };
}

export function hashOf(route: Route): string {
  return `#/${route}`;
}
''')

open('src/web/state/persist.ts','w').write('''import { z } from 'zod';
import { KitchenSchema, ServeTimeSchema, validatePlan } from '../../core/index.js';
import { COPY } from '../copy.js';
import { CookStateSchema } from './cook-slot.js';
import { initialState, reduce } from './store.js';
import type { AppState } from './store.js';

export const STATE_KEY = 'make-dinner:state';

const PlanSourceSchema = z.discriminatedUnion('kind', [
  z.strictObject({ kind: z.literal('sample'), sampleId: z.string().min(1).max(80) }),
  z.strictObject({ kind: z.literal('recipes') }),
  z.strictObject({ kind: z.literal('dinnerfile') }),
  z.strictObject({ kind: z.literal('share') }),
]);

export const PersistedStateSchema = z.strictObject({
  version: z.literal(1),
  route: z.enum(['menu', 'steps', 'plan', 'cook']),
  theme: z.enum(['system', 'light', 'dark']),
  dinner: z
    .strictObject({
      plan: z.unknown(), // validatePlan must give a plan
      source: PlanSourceSchema,
      kitchen: KitchenSchema,
      serving: z.strictObject({ time: ServeTimeSchema, atMs: z.int() }).nullable(),
    })
    .nullable(),
  recipeText: z.string().max(200_000),
  dinnerfileText: z.string().max(1_048_576),
  cook: CookStateSchema.nullable(),
});

export function toPersisted(state: AppState): z.input<typeof PersistedStateSchema> {
  return {
    version: 1,
    route: state.route,
    theme: state.theme,
    dinner:
      state.dinner === null
        ? null
        : {
            plan: state.dinner.plan,
            source: state.dinner.source,
            kitchen: state.dinner.kitchen,
            serving: state.dinner.serving,
          },
    recipeText: state.recipeText,
    dinnerfileText: state.dinnerfileText,
    cook: state.cook,
  };
}

function fresh(): { state: AppState; notice: string } {
  return { state: initialState(), notice: COPY.savedUnreadable };
}

/**
 * Reads the stored state. Anything unreadable starts fresh with a notice; a missing key starts
 * fresh silently. The stored route goes through `navigate`, so an unreachable one is replaced.
 */
export function loadState(
  storage: Pick<Storage, 'getItem'>,
  cookRegistered: boolean,
): { state: AppState; notice: string | null } {
  let raw: string | null;
  try {
    raw = storage.getItem(STATE_KEY);
  } catch {
    return fresh();
  }
  if (raw === null) return { state: initialState(), notice: null };
  let json: unknown;
  try {
    json = JSON.parse(raw);
  } catch {
    return fresh();
  }
  const parsed = PersistedStateSchema.safeParse(json);
  if (!parsed.success) return fresh();
  const stored = parsed.data;
  let dinner: AppState['dinner'] = null;
  if (stored.dinner !== null) {
    const check = validatePlan(stored.dinner.plan);
    if (check.plan === null) return fresh();
    dinner = {
      plan: check.plan,
      source: stored.dinner.source,
      kitchen: stored.dinner.kitchen,
      serving: stored.dinner.serving,
    };
  }
  const loaded: AppState = {
    ...initialState(),
    theme: stored.theme,
    dinner,
    recipeText: stored.recipeText,
    dinnerfileText: stored.dinnerfileText,
    cook: stored.cook,
  };
  const state = reduce(loaded, { type: 'navigate', route: stored.route, cookRegistered });
  return { state, notice: null };
}

/** Writes the persisted state; false when the storage refuses (R9.6). */
export function saveState(storage: Pick<Storage, 'setItem'>, state: AppState): boolean {
  try {
    storage.setItem(STATE_KEY, JSON.stringify(toPersisted(state)));
    return true;
  } catch {
    return false;
  }
}
''')

    #!/usr/bin/env bash
    # Runs after a file is written (agent PostToolUse hook) or saved (IDE PostFileSave hook).
    #
    #   1. npm run typecheck, always.
    #   2. npm run test:props with FC_NUM_RUNS=50, only if a file under src/core, test/core or
    #      test/support changed since the previous firing of this script.
    #
    # Prints the last lines of each step and its exit code. Exits 1 if any step failed, so the
    # agent is told. Appends one line per firing to the hook log. The step limits keep the whole
    # run inside the 60 second hook timeout.
    #
    # Environment overrides: HOOK_LOG, HOOK_STAMP (file whose mtime marks the previous firing),
    # HOOK_TYPECHECK_CMD, HOOK_PROPS_CMD, HOOK_TYPECHECK_SECONDS, HOOK_PROPS_SECONDS.
    
    set -u
    # shellcheck source=lib.sh
    source "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")/lib.sh"
    
    STAMP="${HOOK_STAMP:-$HOOK_ROOT/node_modules/.cache/after-write.stamp}"
    TYPECHECK_CMD="${HOOK_TYPECHECK_CMD:-npm run typecheck}"
    PROPS_CMD="${HOOK_PROPS_CMD:-env FC_NUM_RUNS=50 npm run test:props}"
    TYPECHECK_SECONDS="${HOOK_TYPECHECK_SECONDS:-25}"
    PROPS_SECONDS="${HOOK_PROPS_SECONDS:-25}"
    WATCHED=(src/core test/core test/support)
    
    # Did anything in the watched folders change since the previous firing? No stamp means this
    # is the first firing, which counts as changed.
    changed=no
    if [ ! -f "$STAMP" ]; then
      changed=yes
    exit 0

import AxeBuilder from '@axe-core/playwright';
import { expect } from '@playwright/test';
import type { Page } from '@playwright/test';
import type { Kitchen, Plan, ServeTime } from '../src/core/index.js';

/** The key `src/web/state/persist.ts` stores the dinner under. */
const STATE_KEY = 'make-dinner:state';

/** The fixed clock of the flows: Wednesday 2026-11-11, 17:00 in London (GMT, so UTC). */
export const FIXED_NOW = '2026-11-11T17:00:00Z';

/**
 * Writes a persisted state before the app boots, once per browser context: a reload keeps
 * what the app stored. `serve` is a clock time on the fixed day; London is on GMT then.
 */
export async function seedDinner(
  page: Page,
  plan: Plan,
  kitchen: Kitchen,
  serve: ServeTime | null,
  route: 'menu' | 'steps' | 'plan' = 'plan',
): Promise<void> {
  const state = {
    version: 1,
    route,
    theme: 'system',
    dinner: {
      plan,
      source: { kind: 'sample', sampleId: 'sunday-roast' },
      kitchen,
      serving: serve === null ? null : { time: serve, atMs: Date.parse(`2026-11-11T${serve}:00Z`) },
    },
    recipeText: '',
    dinnerfileText: '',
    cook: null,
  };
  await page.addInitScript(
    ({ key, value }) => {
      if (window.localStorage.getItem(key) === null) window.localStorage.setItem(key, value);
    },
    { key: STATE_KEY, value: JSON.stringify(state) },
  );
}

/** Axe with the three WCAG tag sets of testing.md; zero violations is the pass condition. */
export async function expectNoAxeViolations(page: Page): Promise<void> {
  const results = await new AxeBuilder({ page })
    .withTags(['wcag2a', 'wcag2aa', 'wcag21aa'])
    .analyze();
  expect(results.violations).toEqual([]);
}

/** The page never scrolls sideways (R6.9): the root's scroll width fits its client width. */
export async function expectNoPageScroll(page: Page): Promise<void> {
  const widths = await page.evaluate(() => ({
    scroll: document.documentElement.scrollWidth,
    client: document.documentElement.clientWidth,
  }));
  expect(widths.scroll).toBeLessThanOrEqual(widths.client);
}

    );
  });
});

import { createContext, createElement } from 'preact';
import type { ComponentChildren, VNode } from 'preact';
import { useContext, useEffect, useReducer, useRef } from 'preact/hooks';
import type { Action, AppState, Store } from './store.js';

const StoreContext = createContext<Store | null>(null);

export function StoreProvider(props: { store: Store; children: ComponentChildren }): VNode {
  return createElement(StoreContext.Provider, { value: props.store }, props.children);
}

function useStore(): Store {
  const store = useContext(StoreContext);
  if (store === null) throw new Error('useAppState needs a StoreProvider');
  return store;
}

/** Reads a part of the state; re-renders when the selected value changes (`Object.is`). */
export function useAppState<T>(select: (state: AppState) => T): T {
  const store = useStore();
  const [, rerender] = useReducer((n: number) => n + 1, 0);
  const value = select(store.getState());
  const latest = useRef(value);
  latest.current = value;
  useEffect(
    () =>
      store.subscribe(() => {
        if (!Object.is(select(store.getState()), latest.current)) rerender(0);
      }),
    // `select` is usually an inline function; the subscription only needs the store.
    [store],
  );
  return value;
}

export function useDispatch(): (action: Action) => void {
  const store = useStore();
  return store.dispatch;
}

cat > src/web/state/effects.ts <<'EOF'
import { parseHash, hashOf } from './route.js';
import { saveState } from './persist.js';
import { COPY } from '../copy.js';
import type { AppState, Store } from './store.js';

/** Everything the effects touch outside the store, so they can run against fakes. */
export interface EffectsEnv {
  storage: Pick<Storage, 'setItem'>;
  window: Pick<Window, 'location' | 'history' | 'addEventListener' | 'removeEventListener'>;
  document: Document;
  /** True once cooking-mode has registered its screen (`SCREENS.cook !== null`). */
  cookRegistered: boolean;
}

/** The six fields `PersistedStateSchema` stores, compared by identity. */
function persistedPart(state: AppState): readonly unknown[] {
  return [
    state.route,
    state.theme,
    state.dinner,
    state.recipeText,
    state.dinnerfileText,
    state.cook,
  ];
}

function samePart(a: readonly unknown[], b: readonly unknown[]): boolean {
  return a.every((item, index) => Object.is(item, b[index]));
}

/** Starts persistence, hash sync and the theme attribute. Returns a function that stops them. */
export function startEffects(store: Store, env: EffectsEnv): () => void {
  const { window: win, document: doc } = env;

  // Hash: the boot target first, then both directions of the sync (R9.3).
  const target = parseHash(win.location.hash);
  if (target.kind === 'route') {
    store.dispatch({ type: 'navigate', route: target.route, cookRegistered: env.cookRegistered });
  }
  const replaceHash = (): void => {
    const desired = hashOf(store.getState().route);
    if (win.location.hash !== desired) win.history.replaceState(null, '', desired);
  };
  replaceHash();

  const onHashChange = (): void => {
    const next = parseHash(win.location.hash);
    if (next.kind === 'route' && next.route !== store.getState().route) {
      store.dispatch({ type: 'navigate', route: next.route, cookRegistered: env.cookRegistered });
    }
    replaceHash();
  };
  win.addEventListener('hashchange', onHashChange);

  let saveFailedShown = false;
  let lastPersisted = persistedPart(store.getState());
  let lastRoute = store.getState().route;

  const apply = (): void => {
    const state = store.getState();

    // Route changed by the app: a history entry.
    if (state.route !== lastRoute) {
      lastRoute = state.route;
      if (win.location.hash !== hashOf(state.route)) win.location.hash = hashOf(state.route);
    }

    // Theme: System removes the attribute so prefers-color-scheme decides.
    if (state.theme === 'system') doc.documentElement.removeAttribute('data-theme');
    else doc.documentElement.setAttribute('data-theme', state.theme);

    // Persistence: only when a persisted field changed.
    const part = persistedPart(state);
    if (!samePart(part, lastPersisted)) {
      lastPersisted = part;
      if (!saveState(env.storage, state) && !saveFailedShown) {
        saveFailedShown = true;
        store.dispatch({ type: 'notice/set', text: COPY.saveFailed });
      }
    }
  };

  const unsubscribe = store.subscribe(apply);
  apply();
  // Boot save: the state that was loaded (or just fresh) is written once.
  saveState(env.storage, store.getState());

  return () => {
    unsubscribe();
    win.removeEventListener('hashchange', onHashChange);
  };
}

cat > src/web/components/FlowNav.tsx <<'EOF'
import type { VNode } from 'preact';
import { COPY } from '../copy.js';
import { useAppState } from '../state/hooks.js';
import { hashOf } from '../state/route.js';
import { reachable } from '../state/store.js';
import type { Route } from '../state/store.js';

const ITEMS: ReadonlyArray<{ route: Route; label: string }> = [
  { route: 'menu', label: COPY.navMenu },
  { route: 'steps', label: COPY.navSteps },
  { route: 'plan', label: COPY.navPlan },
  { route: 'cook', label: COPY.navCook },
];

/** Menu, Steps, Plan, Cook as navigation (R9.2). `cookRegistered` is `SCREENS.cook !== null`. */
export function FlowNav(props: { cookRegistered: boolean }): VNode {
  const route = useAppState((state) => state.route);
  const reach = useAppState((state) => reachable(state, props.cookRegistered));
  return (
    <nav class="flow-nav no-print" aria-label={COPY.navLabel}>
      <ol>
        {ITEMS.map((item) => {
          const state = reach[item.route];
          if (!state.ok) {
            return (
              <li key={item.route} class="flow-nav__item flow-nav__item--off">
                <span>{item.label}</span> <span class="flow-nav__reason">{state.reason}</span>
              </li>
            );
          }
          return (
            <li key={item.route} class="flow-nav__item">
              <a
                href={hashOf(item.route)}
                aria-current={item.route === route ? 'step' : undefined}
              >
                {item.label}
              </a>
            </li>
          );
        })}
      </ol>
    </nav>
  );
}

cat > src/web/components/ErrorBoundary.tsx <<'EOF'
import { Component } from 'preact';
import type { ComponentChildren, VNode } from 'preact';
import { COPY } from '../copy.js';

interface Props {
  children: ComponentChildren;
}
interface State {
  failed: boolean;
}

/** Catches a render throw (R10.2): logs to the console only, offers Reload. */
export class ErrorBoundary extends Component<Props, State> {
  override state: State = { failed: false };

  override componentDidCatch(error: unknown): void {
    console.error(error);
    this.setState({ failed: true });
  }

  override render(): VNode {
    if (!this.state.failed) return <>{this.props.children}</>;
    return (
      <main class="screen">
        <h1 tabIndex={-1}>{COPY.errorHeading}</h1>
        <p>{COPY.errorBody}</p>
        <div class="actions">
          <button type="button" class="button button--primary" onClick={() => location.reload()}>
            {COPY.reload}
          </button>
        </div>
      </main>
    );
  }
}
EOF

cat > src/web/app.tsx <<'EOF'
import type { ComponentType, VNode } from 'preact';
import { useEffect, useRef } from 'preact/hooks';
import { ErrorBoundary } from './components/ErrorBoundary.js';
import { FlowNav } from './components/FlowNav.js';
import { Header } from './components/Header.js';
import { LiveNotice } from './components/LiveNotice.js';
import { documentTitle } from './copy.js';
import { COPY } from './copy.js';
import { Menu } from './screens/Menu.js';
import { Plan } from './screens/Plan.js';
import { Steps } from './screens/Steps.js';
import { useAppState } from './state/hooks.js';
import type { Route } from './state/store.js';

/** The screen table. cooking-mode fills `cook` and changes nothing else (R9.9). */
export const SCREENS: Record<Route, ComponentType | null> = {
  menu: Menu,
  steps: Steps,
  plan: Plan,
  cook: null,
};

const SCREEN_NAMES: Record<Route, string> = {
  menu: COPY.navMenu,
  steps: COPY.navSteps,
  plan: COPY.navPlan,
  cook: COPY.navCook,
};

export function App(): VNode {
  const route = useAppState((state) => state.route);
  const Screen = SCREENS[route] ?? Menu;
  const first = useRef(true);

  useEffect(() => {
    document.title = documentTitle(SCREEN_NAMES[route]);
    // Focus goes to the h1 when the screen changes, not on the first paint (R9.4).
    if (first.current) {
      first.current = false;
      return;
    }
    document.querySelector<HTMLElement>('h1')?.focus();
  }, [route]);

  return (
    <ErrorBoundary>
      <div class="app">
        <Header />
        <FlowNav cookRegistered={SCREENS.cook !== null} />
        <LiveNotice />
        <Screen />
      </div>
    </ErrorBoundary>
  );
}
EOF
cat > src/web/main.tsx <<'EOF'
import { render } from 'preact';
import './styles/tokens.css';
import './styles/base.css';
import './styles/components.css';
import { App, SCREENS } from './app.js';
import { StoreProvider } from './state/hooks.js';
import { startEffects } from './state/effects.js';
import { loadState } from './state/persist.js';
import { createStore } from './state/store.js';

const root = document.getElementById('app');
if (root === null) {
  throw new Error('Missing #app element in index.html');
}

/** localStorage can throw on access (privacy modes); the shim makes that a failed read and write. */
function browserStorage(): Pick<Storage, 'getItem' | 'setItem'> {
  try {
    return window.localStorage;
  } catch {
    const blocked = (): never => {
      throw new Error('Storage is not available');
    };
    return { getItem: blocked, setItem: blocked };
  }
}

const storage = browserStorage();
const cookRegistered = SCREENS.cook !== null;
const loaded = loadState(storage, cookRegistered);
const store = createStore(
  loaded.notice === null ? loaded.state : { ...loaded.state, notice: loaded.notice },
);
startEffects(store, { storage, window, document, cookRegistered });

render(
  <StoreProvider store={store}>
    <App />
  </StoreProvider>,
  root,
);
EOF

test.describe('shell: header', () => {
  test.use({ colorScheme: 'light' });

  test('R9.1 theme Dark under a light system sets data-theme and the dark --color-bg, survives a reload, System removes it', async ({
    page,
  }) => {
    await page.goto('/');
    const root = page.locator('html');
    await expect(root).not.toHaveAttribute('data-theme', /.*/);
    await expect(page.locator('body')).toHaveCSS('background-color', 'rgb(246, 241, 231)');

    await page.getByLabel('Theme').selectOption({ label: 'Dark' });
    await expect(root).toHaveAttribute('data-theme', 'dark');
    await expect(page.locator('body')).toHaveCSS('background-color', 'rgb(26, 24, 22)');

    await page.reload();
    await expect(root).toHaveAttribute('data-theme', 'dark');
    await expect(page.getByLabel('Theme')).toHaveValue('dark');
    await expect(page.locator('body')).toHaveCSS('background-color', 'rgb(26, 24, 22)');

    await page.getByLabel('Theme').selectOption({ label: 'System' });
    await expect(root).not.toHaveAttribute('data-theme', /.*/);
    await expect(page.locator('body')).toHaveCSS('background-color', 'rgb(246, 241, 231)');
  });

  test('R9.7 Start over needs a second tap; Cancel returns focus to the button and keeps the dinner', async ({
    page,
  }) => {
    await seedDinner(page, SUNDAY_ROAST, HOME_KITCHEN, '19:30', 'plan');
    await page.goto('/');
    const start = page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Start over' });
    await start.click();
    await expect(
      page.getByText('Start over? This removes your dinner from this device.'),
    ).toBeFocused();
    await expect(page.getByRole('heading', { level: 1 })).toHaveText('Sunday roast');
    await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Cancel' }).click();
    await expect(page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Start over' })).toBeFocused();
    await expect(page.getByRole('heading', { level: 1 })).toHaveText('Sunday roast');
  });

  test('R9.7 confirming Start over opens the Menu, clears the dinner and keeps the theme', async ({
    page,
  }) => {
    await seedDinner(page, SUNDAY_ROAST, HOME_KITCHEN, '19:30', 'plan');
    await page.goto('/');
    await page.getByLabel('Theme').selectOption({ label: 'Dark' });
    await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Start over' }).click();
    await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Yes, start over' }).click();
    await expect(page.getByRole('heading', { level: 1 })).toHaveText('Pick a dinner');
    await expect(page).toHaveURL(/#\/menu$/);
    await expect(page.locator('html')).toHaveAttribute('data-theme', 'dark');
    await expect(
      page.getByRole('navigation', { name: 'Dinner' }).getByRole('link'),
    ).toHaveCount(1);
    await page.reload();
    await expect(page.getByRole('heading', { level: 1 })).toHaveText('Pick a dinner');
    await expect(page.getByLabel('Theme')).toHaveValue('dark');
  });

  test('N1 the header targets are at least 48×48 px', async ({ page }) => {
    await seedDinner(page, SUNDAY_ROAST, HOME_KITCHEN, '19:30', 'plan');
    await page.goto('/');
    const targets = [
      page.getByRole('link', { name: 'Make Dinner' }),
      page.getByLabel('Theme'),
      page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Start over' }),
    ];
    for (const target of targets) {
      const box = await target.boundingBox();
      expect(box?.width).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(48);
      expect(box?.height).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(48);
    }
    await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Start over' }).click();
    for (const name of ['Yes, start over', 'Cancel']) {
      const box = await page.getByRole('button', { name }).boundingBox();
      expect(box?.width).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(48);
      expect(box?.height).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(48);
    }
  });
});

[tool:execute] $ cd ~/hackathons-2026/kiroween/makedinner && cat > src/web/components/ConfirmInline.tsx <<'EOF'
import type { VNode } from 'preact';
import { useEffect, useRef } from 'preact/hooks';

interface Props {
  text: string;
  confirmLabel: string;
  cancelLabel: string;
  onConfirm: () => void;
  onCancel: () => void;
}

/**
 * The second, separate tap that a destructive or replacing action needs (ui.md). Focus moves
 * to the question when it appears; the caller returns focus to its own button on cancel.
 */
export function ConfirmInline(props: Props): VNode {
  const question = useRef<HTMLParagraphElement>(null);
  useEffect(() => {
    question.current?.focus();
  }, []);
  return (
    <div class="confirm-inline" role="group" aria-labelledby="confirm-inline-text">
      <p id="confirm-inline-text" ref={question} tabIndex={-1}>
        {props.text}
      </p>
      <div class="actions">
        <button type="button" class="button button--primary" onClick={props.onConfirm}>
          {props.confirmLabel}
        </button>
        <button type="button" class="button" onClick={props.onCancel}>
          {props.cancelLabel}
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

cat > src/web/components/ThemeSwitch.tsx <<'EOF'
import type { VNode } from 'preact';
import { COPY } from '../copy.js';
import { useAppState, useDispatch } from '../state/hooks.js';
import type { ThemeChoice } from '../state/store.js';

const CHOICES: ReadonlyArray<{ value: ThemeChoice; label: string }> = [
  { value: 'system', label: COPY.themeSystem },
  { value: 'light', label: COPY.themeLight },
  { value: 'dark', label: COPY.themeDark },
];

function isThemeChoice(value: string): value is ThemeChoice {
  return CHOICES.some((choice) => choice.value === value);
}

export function ThemeSwitch(): VNode {
  const theme = useAppState((state) => state.theme);
  const dispatch = useDispatch();
  return (
    <label class="theme-switch">
      <span>{COPY.theme}</span>
      <select
        class="select"
        value={theme}
        onChange={(event) => {
          const value = event.currentTarget.value;
          if (isThemeChoice(value)) dispatch({ type: 'theme/set', theme: value });
        }}
      >
        {CHOICES.map((choice) => (
          <option key={choice.value} value={choice.value}>
            {choice.label}
          </option>
        ))}
      </select>
    </label>
  );
}
EOF
cat > src/web/components/StartOver.tsx <<'EOF'
import type { VNode } from 'preact';
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'preact/hooks';
import { COPY } from '../copy.js';
import { useDispatch } from '../state/hooks.js';
import { ConfirmInline } from './ConfirmInline.js';

/** "Start over" with a separate confirming tap (R9.7). */
export function StartOver(): VNode {
  const dispatch = useDispatch();
  const [asking, setAsking] = useState(false);
  const button = useRef<HTMLButtonElement>(null);
  const cancelled = useRef(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!asking && cancelled.current) {
      cancelled.current = false;
      button.current?.focus();
    }
  }, [asking]);

  if (asking) {
    return (
      <ConfirmInline
        text={COPY.startOverAsk}
        confirmLabel={COPY.startOverYes}
        cancelLabel={COPY.cancel}
        onConfirm={() => {
          setAsking(false);
          dispatch({ type: 'start-over' });
        }}
        onCancel={() => {
          cancelled.current = true;
          setAsking(false);
        }}
      />
    );
  }
  return (
    <button type="button" class="button" ref={button} onClick={() => setAsking(true)}>
      {COPY.startOver}
    </button>
  );
}
EOF

cat > public/theme.js <<'EOF'
// Sets data-theme from the stored choice before the first paint, so a stored Dark or Light
// does not flash the system theme. A classic blocking script in <head>, not inline, so a
// strict Content-Security-Policy still holds. Keep the key in step with src/web/state/persist.ts.
(function () {
  try {
    var stored = JSON.parse(window.localStorage.getItem('make-dinner:state') || 'null');
    var theme = stored && stored.theme;
    if (theme === 'light' || theme === 'dark') {
      document.documentElement.setAttribute('data-theme', theme);
    }
  } catch (error) {
    // Storage blocked or unreadable: the app starts with the system theme.
  }
})();
EOF

open(p,'w').write(s)
EOF

[tool:execute] $ cd ~/hackathons-2026/kiroween/makedinner && cat > test/web/state/api.test.ts <<'EOF'
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { fetchSamples } from '../../../src/web/state/api.js';
import type { FetchLike } from '../../../src/web/state/api.js';
import { HOME_KITCHEN, SUNDAY_ROAST } from '../../support/fixtures.js';

/** One item of `GET /api/samples`, written out field by field (core `SampleItemSchema`). */
function sampleItem(): Record<string, unknown> {
  return {
    id: 'sunday-roast',
    title: 'Sunday roast',
    blurb: 'Roast chicken with gravy and greens.',
    dishes: ['Roast chicken', 'Gravy', 'Spring greens'],
    kitchen: HOME_KITCHEN,
    servingTime: '13:30',
    text: 'Sunday roast\n\nRoast chicken\nRoast it.',
    plan: SUNDAY_ROAST,
    issues: [],
  };
}

function respond(status: number, body: string): FetchLike {
  return () => Promise.resolve(new Response(body, { status }));
}

describe('fetchSamples (R1.1, R1.2)', () => {
  it('R1.1 asks GET /api/samples and returns the items in response order', async () => {
    const calls: string[] = [];
    const second = { ...sampleItem(), id: 'weeknight-curry', title: 'Weeknight curry' };
    const fetchFn: FetchLike = (url) => {
      calls.push(url);
      return Promise.resolve(
        new Response(JSON.stringify({ samples: [sampleItem(), second] }), { status: 200 }),
      );
    };
    const result = await fetchSamples(fetchFn);
    expect(calls).toEqual(['/api/samples']);
    expect(result.ok).toBe(true);
    if (!result.ok) return;
    expect(result.value.map((s) => s.title)).toEqual(['Sunday roast', 'Weeknight curry']);
    expect(result.value[0]?.plan.title).toBe('Sunday roast');
    expect(result.value[0]?.servingTime).toBe('13:30');
    expect(result.value[0]?.kitchen).toEqual({ burners: 4, ovens: 1, cooks: 1 });
  });

  it('R1.2 an extra field is dropped, not fatal', async () => {
    const withExtra = { ...sampleItem(), recordedAt: '2026-11-11' };
    const result = await fetchSamples(
      respond(200, JSON.stringify({ samples: [withExtra], note: 'x' })),
    );
    expect(result.ok).toBe(true);
    if (!result.ok) return;
    expect(Object.keys(result.value[0] ?? {}).sort()).toEqual(
      ['blurb', 'dishes', 'id', 'issues', 'kitchen', 'plan', 'servingTime', 'text', 'title'].sort(),
    );
  });

  it('R1.2 an item without a plan fails the schema: unexpected', async () => {
    const { plan: _plan, ...noPlan } = sampleItem();
    const result = await fetchSamples(respond(200, JSON.stringify({ samples: [noPlan] })));
    expect(result).toEqual({ ok: false, error: 'unexpected' });
  });

  it('R1.2 an empty sample list fails the schema: unexpected', async () => {
    expect(await fetchSamples(respond(200, JSON.stringify({ samples: [] })))).toEqual({
      ok: false,
      error: 'unexpected',
    });
  });

  it('R1.2 bad JSON is unexpected', async () => {
    expect(await fetchSamples(respond(200, '<html>'))).toEqual({
      ok: false,
      error: 'unexpected',
    });
  });

  it('R1.2 a 500 is unexpected, even with a valid body', async () => {
    const body = JSON.stringify({ samples: [sampleItem()] });
    expect(await fetchSamples(respond(500, body))).toEqual({ ok: false, error: 'unexpected' });
  });

  it('R1.2 a thrown fetch is network', async () => {
    const fetchFn: FetchLike = () => Promise.reject(new TypeError('Failed to fetch'));
    expect(await fetchSamples(fetchFn)).toEqual({ ok: false, error: 'network' });
  });
});
EOF

export async function fetchSamples(
  fetchFn: FetchLike,
): Promise<Result<SampleItem[], 'network' | 'unexpected'>> {
  let response: Response;
  try {
    response = await fetchFn('/api/samples');
  } catch {
    return err('network');
  }
  if (!response.ok) return err('unexpected');
  let body: unknown;
  try {
    body = await response.json();
  } catch {
    return err('unexpected');
  }
  const parsed = SamplesResponseSchema.safeParse(body);
  return parsed.success ? ok(parsed.data.samples) : err('unexpected');
}
'''

describe('samples and dinner loading (T2.2)', () => {
  const originalZone = process.env['TZ'];
  beforeAll(() => {
    process.env['TZ'] = 'Europe/London';
    expect(new Date(2026, 6, 1).getTimezoneOffset()).toBe(-60);
  });
  afterAll(() => {
    if (originalZone === undefined) delete process.env['TZ'];
    else process.env['TZ'] = originalZone;
  });

  /** SUNDAY_ROAST x HOME_KITCHEN has span 110 (scheduler table H1). */
  const sampleLoad: DinnerLoad = {
    plan: SUNDAY_ROAST,
    source: { kind: 'sample', sampleId: 'sunday-roast' },
    kitchen: HOME_KITCHEN,
    serve: null,
    suggested: '13:30',
    route: 'plan',
    notice: null,
  };

    const loading = reduce(initialState(), { type: 'samples/loading' });
    expect(loading.samples).toEqual({ status: 'loading' });
    const item = { id: 'sunday-roast' } as unknown as SampleItem;
    const loaded = reduce(loading, { type: 'samples/loaded', samples: [item] });
    expect(loaded.samples).toEqual({ status: 'loaded', samples: [item] });
    expect(reduce(loaded, { type: 'samples/failed' }).samples).toEqual({ status: 'failed' });
  });

  it('R1.3 R1.4 a sample load at Wed 10:00 serves at its suggested 13:30 and opens Plan', () => {
    const next = reduce(initialState(), {
      type: 'dinner/load',
      load: sampleLoad,
      nowMs: at('2026-11-11T10:00:00Z'),
    });
    expect(next.route).toBe('plan');
    expect(next.dinner).toEqual({
      plan: SUNDAY_ROAST,
      source: { kind: 'sample', sampleId: 'sunday-roast' },
      kitchen: HOME_KITCHEN,
      serving: { time: '13:30', atMs: at('2026-11-11T13:30:00Z') },
    });
    expect(next.pendingLoad).toBeNull();
  });

  it('R1.4 at Wed 12:00 13:30 is too soon for span 110 + 15, so it serves at 14:30', () => {
    const next = reduce(initialState(), {
      type: 'dinner/load',
      load: sampleLoad,
      nowMs: at('2026-11-11T12:00:00Z'),
    });
    expect(next.dinner?.serving).toEqual({ time: '14:30', atMs: at('2026-11-11T14:30:00Z') });
  });

  it('R4.11 a load that opens Steps chooses no serving time', () => {
    const next = reduce(initialState(), {
      type: 'dinner/load',
      load: { ...sampleLoad, suggested: null, route: 'steps', notice: 'Opened.' },
      nowMs: at('2026-11-11T10:00:00Z'),
    });
    expect(next.route).toBe('steps');
    expect(next.dinner?.serving).toBeNull();
    expect(next.notice).toBe('Opened.');
  });

  it('R8.5 a fixed serve time is resolved by resolveServing', () => {
    const next = reduce(initialState(), {
      type: 'dinner/load',
      load: { ...sampleLoad, serve: '19:30', suggested: null },
      nowMs: at('2026-11-11T18:00:00Z'),
    });
    expect(next.dinner?.serving).toEqual({ time: '19:30', atMs: at('2026-11-11T19:30:00Z') });
    const tomorrow = reduce(initialState(), {
      type: 'dinner/load',
      load: { ...sampleLoad, serve: '13:59', suggested: null },
      nowMs: at('2026-11-11T18:00:00Z'),
    });
    expect(tomorrow.dinner?.serving).toEqual({ time: '13:59', atMs: at('2026-11-12T13:59:00Z') });
  });

  it('R2.8 a load clears the pasted text of its own source only', () => {
    const base = { ...initialState(), recipeText: 'recipes', dinnerfileText: 'dinnerfile' };
    const fromRecipes = reduce(base, {
      type: 'dinner/load',
      load: { ...sampleLoad, source: { kind: 'recipes' }, route: 'steps' },
      nowMs: 0,
    });
    expect([fromRecipes.recipeText, fromRecipes.dinnerfileText]).toEqual(['', 'dinnerfile']);

      type: 'dinner/load',
      load: { ...sampleLoad, source: { kind: 'dinnerfile' }, route: 'steps' },
      nowMs: 0,
    });
    expect([fromFile.recipeText, fromFile.dinnerfileText]).toEqual(['recipes', '']);
  });

  const withDinner = (): AppState =>
    reduce(initialState(), {
      type: 'dinner/load',
      load: sampleLoad,
      nowMs: at('2026-11-11T10:00:00Z'),
    });
  const otherLoad: DinnerLoad = {
    ...sampleLoad,
    plan: { ...SUNDAY_ROAST, title: 'Another dinner' },
    source: { kind: 'sample', sampleId: 'another' },
    suggested: '19:30',
  };

  it('R1.5 a load while a dinner exists only sets pendingLoad', () => {
    const state = withDinner();
    const next = reduce(state, { type: 'dinner/load', load: otherLoad, nowMs: 0 });
    expect(next).toEqual({ ...state, pendingLoad: otherLoad });
  });

  it('R1.5 replace applies the pending load, clears pendingLoad and the cook slice', () => {
    const cooking = {
      ...reduce(withDinner(), { type: 'dinner/load', load: otherLoad, nowMs: 0 }),
      cook: 'session' as unknown as AppState['cook'],
    };
    const next = reduce(cooking, { type: 'dinner/replace', nowMs: at('2026-11-11T10:00:00Z') });
    expect(next.dinner?.plan.title).toBe('Another dinner');
    expect(next.dinner?.serving).toEqual({ time: '19:30', atMs: at('2026-11-11T19:30:00Z') });
    expect(next.pendingLoad).toBeNull();
    expect(next.cook).toBeNull();
    expect(next.route).toBe('plan');
  });

  it('R1.5 keep drops the pending load and changes nothing else', () => {
    const pending = reduce(withDinner(), { type: 'dinner/load', load: otherLoad, nowMs: 0 });
    const next = reduce(pending, { type: 'dinner/keep' });
    expect(next).toEqual(withDinner());
  });

  it('replace and keep without a pending load are no-ops', () => {
    const state = withDinner();
    expect(reduce(state, { type: 'dinner/replace', nowMs: 0 })).toBe(state);
    expect(reduce(state, { type: 'dinner/keep' })).toBe(state);
  });
});
'''
open(p,'w').write(s)

function spanOf(plan: Plan, kitchen: Kitchen): number {
  const scheduled = schedule(plan, kitchen);
  if (scheduled.ok) return scheduled.value.spanMin;
  const chain = longestChainMinutes(plan);
  return chain.ok ? chain.value : 0;
}

/** Puts a load in place of the dinner: serving time, route, notice and pasted texts (R1.3 to R2.8). */
function applyLoad(state: AppState, load: DinnerLoad, nowMs: number): AppState {
  let serving: Serving | null = null;
  if (load.serve !== null) {
    serving = { time: load.serve, atMs: resolveServing(load.serve, nowMs) };
  } else if (load.route === 'plan') {
    serving = defaultServing(nowMs, spanOf(load.plan, load.kitchen), load.suggested);
  }
  return {
    ...state,
    route: load.route,
    dinner: { plan: load.plan, source: load.source, kitchen: load.kitchen, serving },
    recipeText: load.source.kind === 'recipes' ? '' : state.recipeText,
    dinnerfileText: load.source.kind === 'dinnerfile' ? '' : state.dinnerfileText,
    import: { status: 'idle' },
    pendingLoad: null,
    notice: load.notice,
  };
}

const ROUTE_ORDER""",1)
